## In PR (from pool items)

- [#6836](https://github.com/lamontaes/Political-Game-Git/pull/6836): A door yes holds for the one household the door reached, not the whole counted record ([campaign-door-yes-one-household](items/campaign-door-yes-one-household.md))
- [#6838](https://github.com/lamontaes/Political-Game-Git/pull/6838): A door touches the counted group: the player's evening of doors fills in only who a porch scene could cast ([campaign-doors-touch-the-count](items/campaign-doors-touch-the-count.md))
- [#6849](https://github.com/lamontaes/Political-Game-Git/pull/6849): path-check campaigns every week to election day, files for council first and votes for itself ([path-check-campaigns-to-election-day](items/path-check-campaigns-to-election-day.md))
- [#6846](https://github.com/lamontaes/Political-Game-Git/pull/6846): Issues shows one subject at a time on a rail, its law cards paged in a plate, and every subject reachable ([play-menus-issues-subjects-paged](items/play-menus-issues-subjects-paged.md))
- [#6782](https://github.com/lamontaes/Political-Game-Git/pull/6782): Teens carry a substance-use record in the health engine, so legal cannabis sales reach a named teen ([health-teen-substance-use-record](items/health-teen-substance-use-record.md))
- [#6808](https://github.com/lamontaes/Political-Game-Git/pull/6808): High school graduates enroll in and complete college in the school engine, so higher education spending reaches a named graduate ([school-college-step-record](items/school-college-step-record.md))
- [#6844](https://github.com/lamontaes/Political-Game-Git/pull/6844): Menus sit on framed plates, tabs sit on a rail, and the person card tells a life as a timeline ([play-menus-plates-rail-person-card](items/play-menus-plates-rail-person-card.md))
- [#6850](https://github.com/lamontaes/Political-Game-Git/pull/6850): A territory's laws say territory or commonwealth law, never State law ([play-territory-law-level-words](items/play-territory-law-level-words.md))
